The IB preserves intent-relevant cross-turn attack signals while filtering task-irrelevant and repetitive information, and a lightweight detector identifies malicious memories from the resulting representations. As such, MIND mitigates information redundancy in multi-turn contexts while avoiding the overhead of repeated LLM auditing. Extensive experiments show that MIND reduces attack success rates while preserving task accuracy and inference efficiency. Notably, on ReAct-StrategyQA, MIND reduces mean ASR-r and ASR-a by 55.4% and 55.3%, respectively, while matching the undefended agent in average accuracy and latency.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Rephrasing: Book-Level Organization Improves Synthetic Textbook Data for Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28109)
*Jiawen Tao,Miao Peng,Yaoming Li,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Wenhan Yu,Guoan Wang,Nuo Chen,Tong Yang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究合成教材数据在语言模型预训练中的作用，提出一个可扩展的合成流水线（检索、聚类、规划目录、组装成完整书籍），并通过控制实验证明"书籍级组织"这一结构因素比内容生成本身更重要，能显著提升下游任务平均性能（+1.09至+1.17）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于合成教科书数据的研究多聚焦于生成内容的质量或局部改写风格，而忽视了文档组织结构（是否将相关内容组织成连贯的书籍级文档）这一潜在关键因素。作者希望分离并验证"文档组织"这一独立维度对预训练性能的贡献。

Method: 提出可扩展的合成流水线：从预训练语料库检索源材料→按主题聚类→规划分层目录（TOC）→将基于源材料的章节组装为完整书籍（Full条件），共生成686K本教科书（32B tokens），覆盖15,000+学科。设计多重控制实验：Split条件（内容匹配但每章节视为独立文档，隔离文档打包效应）、RandomConcat条件（长度匹配但章节来自不同书籍，排除文档长度因素）、Rephrase条件（独立改写检索文档，无聚类/TOC/组装）。

Result: 在预训练中期混合训练中，用合成书籍替换自然书籍使下游任务平均提升+1.09；Full相对Split提升+1.02（隔离纯文档打包因素）；Full相对RandomConcat表现更优（排除仅文档长度因素）；Full相对Rephrase提升+1.17（证明结构化合成的价值）；在Llama3-8B上Full同样优于RandomConcat和自然书籍。

Conclusion: 书籍级组织（book-level organization）是合成预训练数据设计中的一个有用且独立的设计轴，相较单纯的内容生成或改写，将相关内容结构化组织成连贯书籍能带来更稳定的性能增益。

Abstract: Synthetic textbook data has improved language model pre-training, but prior work largely treats the benefit as a property of generated content or local rewriting style. We study a different factor: whether related content is organized into coherent book-level documents. We contribute both a scalable synthesis pipeline and controlled evidence that this organization matters. The pipeline retrieves source material from a pre-training corpus, clusters it into topical units, plans hierarchical tables of contents, and assembles source-grounded sections into complete books (our Full setting), yielding 686K textbooks (32B tokens) across 15,000+ disciplines. Replacing natural books in a mid-training mix with this corpus improves downstream performance by +1.09 on average. Controlled comparisons then disentangle the relevant design factors. A content-matched Split condition holds generated text and tokens fixed but treats each section as an independent document; Full's +1.02 mean gain isolates document packaging. A length-matched RandomConcat control that joins sections from different books remains below Full, ruling out document length alone. A retrieval-pool-matched Rephrase condition independently rewrites individual retrieved documents under the same audience-by-style scheme, without clustering, TOC planning, or book assembly; Full's +1.17 gain demonstrates the value of structured synthesis. On Llama3-8B, Full likewise outperforms both RandomConcat and Natural Books, supporting book-level organization as a useful axis for synthetic pre-training data design.

</details>


### [101] [BlueprintRepair: Typed Local Edits for Failed Lean Proof Blueprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28110)
*Ruslan Khrulev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BlueprintRepair，一个基于蓝图（证明依赖图）的LLM证明系统修复接口，支持十种经模式检查的局部编辑操作，并构建了包含142个受控失败及完整轨迹的BlueprintTrace基准。在相同资源下对比三种修复接口发现，类型化修复在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上最便宜且覆盖收敛最快，在Qwen3.6-Flash上同样保持成本优势并在证明编写类状态上领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的Lean证明系统将证明组织为蓝图（形式化语句的依赖图），现有修复方式缺乏结构化的、可验证的接口。需要一种既能定位目标定理、又经过Lean类型检查、且能声明全部依赖引理的修复机制，并需要在受控的失败场景下系统评估不同修复接口的效率与覆盖能力。

Method: 提出了BlueprintRepair修复接口，允许模型通过十种经schema检查的局部操作修改蓝图；每种操作显式指明所编辑的节点以确保目标定理不可被篡改；所有修改须通过Lean的检查，且被接受的修复必须声明其证明用到的所有蓝图中引理。构建BlueprintTrace基准，包含142个受控失败及完整的接受/拒绝修复轨迹。在匹配的源码、反馈、模型与预算下，逐状态、逐接口对比类型化编辑、精确源码补丁、完整模块重写三种接口。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上，三种接口解决的局部化失败数量大致相同；类型化修复每解决一个状态成本最低（补丁为1.30倍，重写为2.06倍），且在10,000个完成token预算内几乎达到其最终覆盖，而两种自由形式接口进展明显落后。第二个模型Qwen3.6-Flash解决的失败较少，但类型化修复依然最便宜，且在证明编写类状态上领先，并复现了局部化模式。

Conclusion: 类型化蓝图修复在性价比与覆盖收敛速度上优于自由形式的源码补丁和模块重写，且该优势在多个模型上具有一致性。细粒度的、经类型检查的结构化编辑是LLM证明系统修复的高效范式。

Abstract: LLM-based Lean proving systems increasingly organize a proof as a blueprint: a dependency graph of formal statements. We introduce BlueprintRepair, a repair interface that lets a model change this graph through ten schema-checked local operations. An operation names the node it edits, so the target theorem cannot be changed. Lean checks every applied change, and an accepted repair must declare every blueprint lemma its proof uses. We also construct BlueprintTrace, a benchmark of 142 controlled failures with complete accepted and rejected repair trajectories. We compare typed edits, exact source patches, and complete module rewrites under matched source, feedback, model, and budget, one episode per state and interface. With DeepSeek-V4-Flash, the three interfaces solve almost the same number of the benchmark's localized failures. Typed repair is the cheapest per solved state (patching is 1.30x as expensive, rewriting 2.06x), and within 10,000 completion tokens per task it reaches almost all of its final coverage, while both free-form interfaces are well behind. A second model, Qwen3.6-Flash, solves fewer states but keeps typed repair cheapest, puts it ahead on the proof-authoring states, and repeats the localized pattern.

</details>


### [102] [ConMem: Contribution-Aware Memory for Long-Horizon Manufacturing Inspection Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28126)
*Bingchen Liu,Yuanyuan Fang,Lei Liu,Guangyuan Dong,Xing Fu,Yuanyuan Gao,Shuyue Wei,Xin Li,Xiangtian Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ConMem，一种贡献感知的记忆框架，用于大语言模型辅助的设备检修。它通过功能片段划分、Shapley式贡献估计和记忆预算约束下的高价值证据保留，实现了长期钢铁设备检修中早期风险信号的保留与报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期钢铁设备检修需要在多次检修周期中推理异构记录。现有RAG系统将历史日志视为静态语料库，保留所有记录而不估计其诊断价值，导致无法报告早期风险信号。

Method: ConMem包含三个步骤：(1) 将检修日志分割为功能性证据单元；(2) 通过Shapley式估计评估每个记忆单元对下游诊断的贡献；(3) 在受限记忆预算下保留高价值证据。整个系统支持人在回路的早期风险筛查。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上，ConMem实现76.0%的QA准确率，超越最强的直接可比基线。相比朴素8K上下文LLM基线，平均输入token数减少88.2%，响应时间减少86.6%。消融实验表明功能角色感知分割和贡献估值有助于优先处理微弱退化信号。实际部署证实ConMem能在三个检修周期中保留微弱早期信号，为现场检查人员提供早期密封磨损预警。

Conclusion: ConMem通过贡献感知的记忆管理有效解决了长期检修场景中历史记录诊断价值不确定的问题，在提升QA准确性的同时大幅降低计算开销，并能稳定保留跨周期的早期风险信号，为现场检修提供实用价值。

Abstract: Long-horizon steel-equipment inspection requires reasoning over heterogeneous records accumulated across repeated inspection cycles. Existing retrieval-augmented generation systems treat historical logs as a static corpus and retain records without estimating their diagnostic value, failing to report early risk. To this end, we propose ConMem, a contribution-aware memory framework for LLM-assisted equipment inspection, supporting a human-in-the-loop early-risk screening system. Specifically, our ConMem first segments inspection logs into functional evidence units, then estimates each memory unit's contribution to downstream diagnosis through a Shapley-style estimation, and finally retains high-value evidence under a constrained memory budget. In experiments, we evaluate ConMem on real-world dataset and ConMem achieves 76.0% QA accuracy, exceeding the strongest directly comparable baseline. Relative to the naive 8K-context LLM baselines, it reduces the average number of input tokens by 88.2% and response time by 86.6%. Ablation studies also show that the functional-role-aware segmentation and contribution-based valuation are helping prioritize weak degradation signals for targeted field inspection. Practical deployments further confirm that ConMem retains the weak early signal across three inspection cycles, providing an early-stage seal-wear alert targeted for on-site inspectors.

</details>


### [103] [AgenticASR: Refining Speech Recognition in Real-World Scenarios via an Agentic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28175)
*Zixuan Jiang,Binghao Qiang,Jiaying Chi,Yanqiao Zhu,Kai Yu,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticSR任务和AgenticASR系统，通过ASR-Refiner架构在音频流中持续将逐字转录文本修正为去除口语非流利、保留说话者最终意图的规范化书面文本，并引入双语基准AASR-Bench进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逐字转录（verbatim transcription）虽然准确率高，但保留了填充词、重复、错误起始和自我修正等内容，导致文本可读性差、难以理解说话者的最终意图，并会把未解决或放弃的内容传播到下游任务。已有的口语转书面方法只能处理完整的音频或转录文本，无法在后续语音改变前文语义解释时对已输出的文本进行修订。

Method: 提出AgenticSR任务（音频到清洁文本），通过ASR-Refiner架构实现：将完成的转录文本反复转换有界活动上下文，并在新音频到达时替换对应的输出片段，从而支持任意时长音频流上的持续输出与修订。同时构建双语基准AASR-Bench，包含细粒度原子评分标准（atomic rubrics），并采用人工-人工智能一致性研究验证评分可靠性。

Result: 在多个ASR前端下，AgenticASR在AASR-Bench上取得最高评分；人类-AI一致性研究表明基于规则的评分与独立专家评估一致；消融实验揭示了Refiner容量、上下文长度以及在线与离线推理之间的质量-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: AgenticASR被验证为一个实用框架，能够在语音持续播报过程中实现保留意图的清洁转录，兼具在线修订能力和良好的评测表现。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has achieved substantial gains in transcription accuracy, yet verbatim transcription does not necessarily produce readily usable text. It retains fillers, repetitions, false starts, and self-corrections that increase reading effort, obscure the speaker's final intent, and propagate unresolved or abandoned content to downstream tasks. Existing spoken-to-written methods process completed audio or transcripts but cannot revise emitted text when later speech changes how preceding content should be interpreted. We therefore formulate Agentic Speech Recognition (AgenticSR), an audio-to-clean-text task that removes disfluencies, resolves self-corrections, and normalizes written form while preserving the speaker's final intent. AgenticASR implements this task through an ASR--Refiner architecture that repeatedly transforms a bounded active context and replaces its corresponding output span as audio arrives. This enables continual emission and revision over streams of arbitrary duration. We also introduce AASR-Bench, a bilingual benchmark with fine-grained atomic rubrics. Across multiple ASR front ends, AgenticASR attains the highest AASR-Bench scores among evaluated systems. A human--AI agreement study shows that rubric-based judgments align with independent expert assessments. Ablations characterize Refiner capacity, context length, and the quality--latency trade-off between online and offline inference. Together, these results establish AgenticASR as a practical framework for intent-preserving clean transcription during ongoing speech. Code, AASR-Bench, and a demo will be released at https://github.com/AnXMuy/AgenticASR.

</details>


### [104] [Qwen-UI-Agent Technical Report: Toward Next-Generation Real-World Centric Foundation GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28227)
*Hanzhang Zhou,Panrong Tong,Xu Zhang,Quyu Kong,Chenglin Cai,Tianyu Xia,Gongjie Zhang,Jianan Zhang,Long Li,Long Chen,Lei Wang,Gaole Dai,Pengxiang Li,Liangyu Chen,Yue Wang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Qwen-UI-Agent，一个以真实世界为中心的通用GUI智能体，覆盖移动端、计算机端、网页和DeepSearch环境，结合统一动作空间（GUI操作与CLI执行交错）、AutoResearch式数据飞轮以及在线强化学习（支持超过100轮轨迹、1万+并发环境），在移动端基准上取得SOTA性能，并在计算机和浏览器任务上达到与前沿模型相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动GUI智能体走向真实世界应用，需要其在真实设备上可靠运行、跨平台执行工作流、结合GUI与CLI、完成长时程任务、主动发起服务并自主提升能力。现有模型在这些方面仍有不足，缺乏面向真实场景的通用基础GUI智能体。

Method: 1) 集成多样沙盒环境与大规模真实设备移动运行时；2) 统一动作空间，在单次模型生成中交错执行GUI操作与CLI命令并批量输出动作；3) 采用AutoResearch式数据飞轮，用智能体构建任务与环境、诊断失败并规划迭代；4) 在线RL支持超过100轮轨迹训练，依托10000+并发环境加速数据采集；5) 轻量级harness层支持主动服务发起及移动端与计算机间的有状态工作流。

Result: 移动端：MobileWorld 82.1%、MobileWorld-Real 92.2%、AndroidDaily 97.5%；计算机端：OSWorld-Verified 79.5%、OSWorld-v2部分完成度40.0%；浏览器与GUI定位：WebArena 73.6%、ScreenSpot-Pro 81.5%。在移动端基准上取得SOTA，在计算机和浏览器任务上与Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.6 Sol等前沿模型竞争。

Conclusion: Qwen-UI-Agent验证了通过结合真实设备环境、统一动作空间、自动化数据飞轮和大规模在线RL，可以构建出面向真实世界、跨平台且具备自主演进能力的通用GUI智能体，为GUI智能体的实际部署奠定基础。

Abstract: GUI agents have the potential to become a general purpose executor over existing digital devices. To advance them toward real-world use, we envision agents that operate reliably on real devices, execute workflows across platforms, combine GUI interaction with CLI execution, complete long-horizon tasks, proactively initiate useful services, and autonomously improve their capabilities with minimal human effort. Guided by this vision, we present Qwen-UI-Agent, a real-world centric foundation GUI agent spanning mobile, computer-use, web, and DeepSearch environments. Qwen-UI-Agent combines diverse sandbox environments with a large-scale real-device mobile runtime. Its unified action space interleaves GUI operations with CLI execution and generates batched actions in a single model turn. An AutoResearch-style data flywheel uses agents to construct tasks and environments, diagnose failures, and plan subsequent iterations. Online RL supports training on trajectories exceeding 100 turns, with over 10,000 concurrent environments accelerating rollout. A lightweight harness layer supports proactive service initiation and stateful workflows across mobile and computer.
  Across a broad suite of evaluations, Qwen-UI-Agent sets state-of-the-art performance on mobile-use benchmarks while delivering competitive performance on computer- and browser-use tasks against frontier models, including Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.6 Sol. On mobile use, it achieves 82.1% on MobileWorld, 92.2% on MobileWorld-Real, and 97.5% on AndroidDaily. On computer use, it achieves 79.5% on OSWorld-Verified and a 40.0% partial-progress score on OSWorld-v2. On browser use and GUI grounding, it achieves 73.6% on WebArena and 81.5% on ScreenSpot-Pro, respectively.

</details>


### [105] [CDAE: Enhancing Perturbation Robustness in Pretrained Language Models with Contrastive Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28236)
*Sina Heydari,Amirreza Abbasi,Mohsen Hooshmand,Majid Ramezani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的对比去噪自编码器（CDAE），通过联合优化对比学习和重构目标来精炼预训练BERT嵌入，使其对同义词替换、掩蔽和词丢弃等语义保持型扰动更具鲁棒性。实验表明，CDAE在多种扰动策略下始终比原始BERT嵌入和SimCSE保持更高的嵌入相似度，且改进幅度随扰动增强而更加显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型显著提升了句子表示学习，但其嵌入对同义词替换、掩蔽和词丢弃等语义保持型文本扰动仍然敏感，导致嵌入稳定性不足，需要一种轻量级方法在保持语义信息的同时实现扰动不变性。

Method: 提出对比去噪自编码器（CDAE），在预训练BERT嵌入基础上，通过联合优化对比学习目标（增强扰动不变性）和重构目标（保留语义信息）来精炼嵌入表示，并对不同强度的多种扰动策略进行系统评估。

Result: 在多种扰动策略和强度下，CDAE始终比原始BERT嵌入和SimCSE保持更高的嵌入相似度，且改进幅度随着扰动增强而更加显著，有效增强了表示稳定性同时保留语义信息。

Conclusion: CDAE验证了扰动不变性学习是提升句子嵌入质量的有前景方向，为预训练模型的嵌入稳定性增强提供了轻量有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Pre-trained language models have significantly improved sentence representation learning, yet their embedding remain sensitive to semantic preserving textual perturbations such as synonym substitution, masking and word dropout. This work proposes a lightweight Contrastive Denoising Autoencoder (CDAE) that refines pre-trained BERT embedding by jointly optimizing contrastive and reconstruction objective to learn perturbation-invariant representation. We evaluate the proposed framework using multiple perturbation strategies with varying strengths and compare it against the original BERT embeddings and SimCSE. Experimental results show that CDAE consistently preserves higher embedding similarity under perturbations, with the improvements becoming more pronounced as framework effectively enhances representation stability while preserving semantic information, highlighting perturbation-invariant learning as a promising direction for improving sentence embeddings. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/ComputationIASBS/CDAE

</details>


### [106] [AI and Authenticity in Islamic Research: A Critical Evaluation of Generative AI Reliability, Hallucination, and Source Fidelity in Quranic, Hadith, and Fiqh Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28237)
*Muhammad Sajjad Akbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对六种主流生成式AI系统进行了实证评估，使用50个涵盖古兰经注释、圣训、教法学、伦理、教牧建议及麦兹哈布（教法学派）敏感议题的现实开放性伊斯兰问题，跨澳大利亚和英国的真实条件下收集回应，采用混合方法框架分析领域准确性、引文验证、幻觉、教法一致性、不确定性处理、来源溯源及地域差异。结论指出：当前生成式AI可作为伊斯兰入门学习的辅助工具，但不应被视为宗教裁决或学术研究的权威来源，需对照经认证的原始资料和合格学者专业意见进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI在穆斯林宗教指导中被越来越多地使用，但缺乏关于其是否提供真实、可验证和可信的伊斯兰知识的实证证据，尤其是在高信任度的宗教语境中。

Method: 采用混合方法框架，对六种主流生成式AI系统提出50个现实开放性伊斯兰问题（涵盖多个知识领域），在澳大利亚和英国的真实条件下收集回应，并分析领域准确性、引文验证、幻觉、教法一致性、不确定性处理、来源溯源和地域差异。

Result: 研究发现当前生成式AI系统在伊斯兰知识方面更适合作为介绍性学习的辅助工具，而非权威宗教来源；其在宗教裁决、伊斯兰研究等场景中需要对照原始资料和专家意见进行验证。该研究是伊斯兰知识领域AI可靠性少数综合性实证评估之一。

Conclusion: 生成式AI在伊斯兰知识领域尚未达到可作为权威参考的程度，仅适合作为辅助学习工具；研究为研究者、教育者、AI开发者和穆斯林社群提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly used by Muslims for religious guidance, Qur'anic interpretation, Hadith explanation, jurisprudential rulings, and Islamic education. Despite its growing adoption, there is limited empirical evidence on whether current AI systems provide authentic, verifiable, and trustworthy Islamic knowledge suitable for high-trust religious contexts. This study evaluates six leading generative AI systems using fifty realistic open-ended Islamic questions covering Qur'anic interpretation, Hadith, Fiqh, ethics, pastoral advice, and Madhhab-sensitive topics. Responses were collected under real-world conditions from participants in Australia and the United Kingdom and analysed using a mixed-method framework examining domain accuracy, citation verification, hallucinations, jurisprudential consistency, uncertainty handling, source provenance, and geographical variation.
  The study addresses four research questions: (1) How accurate and authentic are AI-generated responses across major Islamic knowledge domains? (2) To what extent do AI systems produce hallucinations, incomplete citations, or unverifiable religious references? (3) How consistently do models handle jurisprudential disagreement, Madhhab diversity, and uncertainty? (4) Are current AI systems sufficiently reliable for religious guidance, Islamic education, and scholarly research?
  Overall, current generative AI systems are valuable as assistive tools for introductory Islamic learning but should not be treated as authoritative sources for religious rulings or Islamic research without verification against authenticated primary sources and qualified scholarly expertise. This study provides one of the first comprehensive empirical evaluations of AI reliability within Islamic knowledge, offering practical guidance for researchers, educators, AI developers, and the wider Muslim community.

</details>


### [107] [Operationally Guided Placement-Aware Learning for Industrial Online 3D Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28257)
*Dheeraj Poolavaram,Aanchal Rajesh Chugh,Sebastian Dorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPAL是一个面向工业在线三维装箱问题（3D-BPP）的运营引导且考虑放置感知的学习框架，通过结合运营引导的空最大空间生成器（OG-EMS）、基于xLSTM的放置编码器和带掩码的排序策略（使用PPO训练），在BED-BPP基准上达到0.49的平均空间利用率，相比基线提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线3D-BPP学习方法主要聚焦于优化放置策略本身，而候选生成器和表示方法仍主要依赖几何信息驱动，未充分考虑工业场景中对空间高效、稳定、紧凑和平衡包装的实际操作需求，导致性能受限。

Method: OPAL框架包含三个核心组件：（1）OG-EMS生成器，在自由空间区域内评估多个锚点，优先选择低、良好支撑、紧凑且空间多样化的放置位置；（2）基于xLSTM的放置编码器，建模候选放置的几何和操作属性之间的依赖关系；（3）轻量级循环核心，将嵌入与当前物品和托盘状态结合以排序可行动作，并使用近端策略优化（PPO）训练带掩码的排序策略。

Result: 在BED-BPP基准上，OPAL达到0.49的平均空间利用率。其中，运营引导的候选生成贡献了15.1%的改进，学习排序贡献了6.3%的改进，同时保持了稳健的推理时间性能。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在在线3D-BPP中同时优化候选生成和表示方法（而不仅是策略）能带来显著的性能提升，验证了将操作性引导融入候选生成和表征的有效性。

Abstract: The online three-dimensional bin packing problem (3D-BPP) is a longstanding challenge in logistics and industrial palletizing. Recent learning-based methods use a learned policy to select among feasible candidate placements. Performance depends on the candidate generator and representation, especially in industrial settings where packings must be space-efficient, stable, compact, and balanced. However, prior work has mainly optimized the policy, while candidate generation and representation remain largely geometry-driven. We address this gap with OPAL, an operationally guided placement-aware learning framework for industrial online 3D-BPP which combines an Operationally Guided Empty-Maximal-Space generator (OG-EMS), an operational representation for each candidate placement, and a masked ranking policy trained with proximal policy optimization. OG-EMS evaluates multiple anchors within each free-space region and prioritizes low, well-supported, compact, and spatially diverse placements. An xLSTM-based Placement Encoder models dependencies among geometric and operational candidate attributes, while a lightweight recurrent core combines the resulting embeddings with the current item and pallet state to rank feasible actions. On the BED-BPP benchmark, OPAL achieves a mean space utilization of 0.49, with improvements of 15.1% from operationally guided candidate generation and 6.3% from learned ranking, while maintaining robust inference-time performance.

</details>


### [108] [LLM-Guided Evolutionary Search for Constraint Model Reformulation to Improve Solver Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28268)
*Kostis Michailidis,Dimos Tsouros,Nguyen Dang,Tias Guns*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型（LLM）在进化框架下自动进行性能导向的约束模型重构，提出了一种名为Profile-Diverse Retention (PDR)的上下文保留策略，通过最大边际相关性（MMR）在实例级运行时向量上进行行为多样性筛选，实验表明该方法在8个CSPLib问题上显著提升模型求解速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题在工业应用中广泛存在，通常通过声明式约束模型求解。虽然LLM已能生成正确模型，但模型性能对建模选择高度敏感，即使正确的模型也可能求解代价高昂，因此需要自动化地进行性能导向的模型重构优化。

Method: 借鉴自动启发式设计（AHD）思路，采用进化框架，由LLM提出候选重构方案，通过验证和基准测试对比用户定义的基线模型。比较多种AHD适配的搜索策略控制进入提示的先前尝试、指令和反馈；提出Profile-Diverse Retention (PDR)策略，利用MMR对实例级运行时向量进行行为多样性保留。基于验证集进行最终模型选择。

Result: 在8个CSPLib问题上系统评估表明：(i)迭代重构可产生显著的保留集外加速；(ii)保持上下文多样性的策略优于仅保留最近或最快尝试的策略；(iii)基于验证集的选择可提升所有策略的保留集外加速效果。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的进化框架能够有效自动化约束模型的性能优化重构，其中上下文多样性的保持是提升搜索效率和最终模型性能的关键因素，验证集选择机制进一步增强了模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Combinatorial problems appear in numerous industrial applications. A common approach is to formulate these problems as declarative constraint models that can subsequently be compiled to and solved by a range of back-end solvers. Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can produce correct models from natural language, but even a correct model can be expensive to solve because performance remains sensitive to modelling choices. In this work, we investigate whether LLMs can automate performance-oriented model reformulation. Inspired by Automatic Heuristic Design (AHD), we use an evolutionary framework in which an LLM proposes candidate reformulations that are verified and benchmarked against the user-defined baseline model. We compare AHD-adapted search strategies that control which prior attempts, instructions, and measured feedback enter each prompt. Existing retention strategies prioritize recency or performance, but do not explicitly diversify the context. To cover this gap, we introduce Profile-Diverse Retention (PDR), which applies Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) to instance-level runtime vectors to retain behaviourally diverse attempts. We systematically evaluate the strategies on eight CSPLib problems using validation-based final model selection. The results show that: (i) iterative reformulation can produce substantial held-out speedups; (ii) strategies that keep the retained context diverse outperform those that retain only recent or the fastest attempts; and (iii) validation-based selection improves the held-out speedup of every strategy.

</details>


### [109] [MemHarness: Memory Is Reconstructed, Not Replayed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28272)
*Rong Wu,Daocheng Fu,Licheng Wen,Xuemeng Yang,Shu Zou,Jianbiao Mei,Yuxin Wang,Hairong Zhang,Yu Yang,Tao Hu,Cong Zhang,Botian Shi,Pinlong Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemHarness是一个让LLM智能体主动重构而非简单回放过往经验的框架。它通过统一策略模型在当前状态下批判性地重构检索到的经验，产生情境化的行动指导，并通过GRPO端到端训练习得这一重构能力。在ALFWorld和WebShop实验中显著优于纯RL和静态记忆增强基线，尤其在OOD场景下表现出强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强智能体将检索到的经验视为静态记录并逐字回放，忽略了存储经验的抽象通用性与决策时具体多变状态之间的差距，常导致负迁移。人类回忆过往经验时并非逐字重放，而是根据当前情境重组和调整记忆，这一认知原理启发了一种新的记忆利用范式。

Method: 提出MemHarness框架，在每个决策步骤中，统一策略模型基于当前状态对检索到的经验进行批判性评估和重构，生成情境化的行动指导。该重构能力通过GRPO（组相对策略优化）进行端到端训练自然涌现，无需额外的监督信号。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，MemHarness显著优于纯强化学习和静态记忆增强基线，在OOD场景中表现出强鲁棒性。分析还表明重构目标不仅能防止负迁移，还在训练过程中充当潜在引导，从本质上提升了智能体的内在推理能力。

Conclusion: 从"回放"到"重构"的记忆利用范式转变，能够更有效地弥合存储经验与当前情境之间的差距，同时重构目标在训练中起到隐式引导作用，从根本上改善智能体的推理能力，为记忆增强型LLM智能体提供了新的设计方向。

Abstract: Retrieving past experiences has become a common strategy to enhance large language model agents. However, most existing memory-augmented agents treat retrieved experiences as static records to be replayed verbatim, injecting them into the context regardless of whether they align with the agent's current situation. This ``replay'' paradigm ignores the gap between the abstract, general nature of stored experience and the concrete, ever-changing states encountered at decision time, frequently causing negative transfer. In contrast, humans rarely recall past experiences verbatim; instead, they reorganize and adapt retrieved memories to fit the present context. Inspired by this, we propose MemHarness, a framework that equips LLM agents to actively harness and reconstruct past experiences based on the present context. At each decision step, a unified policy model critiques and reconstructs the retrieved experience conditioned on the current state, producing context-grounded guidance before acting. This reconstructive ability emerges naturally through end-to-end training with GRPO. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that MemHarness substantially outperforms pure RL and static memory-augmented baselines, demonstrating strong robustness in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Furthermore, our analyses reveal that this reconstruction objective not only prevents negative transfer but also serves as latent guidance during training, fundamentally improving the agent's intrinsic reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [110] [Tycho: Active Abstraction with Programmatic World Models for ARC-AGI-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28287)
*Jens Lehmann,Andrei Aioanei,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARC-AGI-3基准，将抽象推理转化为交互式技能获取问题，并引入Tycho编码代理系统，该系统在游戏交互中构建并使用游戏特定模型。在25个公开游戏上对比四种编排策略，基于Claude Opus 4.8执行，最佳策略（Actor委托给模型构建器）达到88.49的平均相对人类行动效率（RHAE）；GPT-5.6 Sol和Opus 5均达到100.00 RHAE并完成全部183关，其中Opus 5比官方人类基线少用61%的计分动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统抽象推理任务多为静态的，而ARC-AGI-3将其扩展为交互式问题：玩家必须在未知游戏规则、隐藏状态和目标下高效行动（每次移动都算成本）。这要求系统不仅能够推断抽象概念，还需在资源受限的交互中平衡探索与利用，因此需要一个统一的框架来评估和推动交互式技能获取能力。

Method: Tycho系统核心设计包括：(1) 将环境形式化为参数化渲染的确定性Moore机；(2) 将可操作观察与中间动画、关卡完成和游戏结束帧分离；(3) 基于结构化历史，代理可建模、测试、规划、修复或绕过自由格式的可执行假设；(4) 定义"主动抽象"问题——从代价高昂的交互中生成可测试模型，并决定何时构建、修复、使用或绕过模型的成本效益权衡。实验对比四种编排策略（包括Actor请求委派给模型构建器），在匹配的推理预算下评估RHAE。

Result: Actor请求委派给模型构建器的策略在Claude Opus 4.8上获得最高平均RHAE 88.49。自动修复（验证失败后修复模型）虽然能更准确地复现观测转移动态，但RHAE仅为83.07，说明转移动态匹配并不等同于识别目标或改进下一动作。采用选定策略后，GPT-5.6 Sol和Opus 5均达到100.00 RHAE并完成全部183关，游戏均衡的首次运行人类回放中位数分别为98.5和100.0；Opus 5比官方人类基线少用61%的计分动作。

Conclusion: 构建精确的模型（高转换匹配）并不自动带来更好的游戏表现，关键在于主动抽象能力——在正确时机决定是构建、修复、使用还是绕过模型。模型是否准确复现观测动态，与是否识别游戏目标或优化下一行动是两个不同维度。高效交互式推理需要在测试模型的价值与其获取成本之间做出明智权衡。

Abstract: ARC-AGI-3 turns abstraction into an interactive problem of skill acquisition. A player must infer an unfamiliar game's rules, hidden state, and goal while maintaining action efficiency because every move counts. We formalize these environments as parameterized rendered deterministic Moore machines and introduce Tycho, a coding-agent system that constructs and uses game-specific models during interaction. Tycho separates actionable observations from intermediate animation, level-completion, and game-over frames. From this structured history, an agent can model, test, plan with, repair, or bypass a free-form executable hypothesis.
  In one matched public-set run per policy, we compare four orchestration policies on all 25 public games using Claude Opus 4.8 under matched inference budgets. Actor-requested delegation to a model builder obtains the highest observed mean Relative Human Action Efficiency (RHAE), 88.49. With this selected policy, GPT-5.6 Sol and Opus 5 both reach 100.00 RHAE and complete all 183 levels. Their game-balanced first-run human-replay midranks are 98.5 and 100.0. Opus 5 uses 61% fewer scored actions than the aggregate official human baselines.
  Automatic repair after verification failures produces models that reproduce observed transitions much more accurately, yet reaches only 83.07 RHAE. Transition match indicates whether a simulator reproduces observed dynamics, not whether it has identified the objective or improves the next action. Strong play also requires deciding when to construct, repair, use, or bypass a model. We call this joint problem active abstraction: generating a testable model from costly interaction and deciding when acquiring or using it is worth its cost.

</details>


### [111] [One Human, $N$ Agents: Audit-Budget Allocation for LLM Agent Fleets under Miscalibrated, Correlated Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28317)
*Cesare Zavattari,Alessandro Tommasi,Giuseppe Prencipe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了单一人类审计者在有限预算下对N个LLM代理进行审计的问题，通过两层高斯copula模型分析预算约束下的噪声检查，找到了置信度排序审计劣于随机审计的误校准阈值δ*，并揭示两个反直觉结论：预算越少δ*越高，跨家族相关性不低（共享难度主导）。五个开源LLM的置信度近乎恒定、操作性无效，专有模型则信息丰富且在阈值之下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限的审计预算下（B ≪ N），人类审计者依赖LLM代理的自报置信度来优先审计，但置信度可能被对抗性误校准，且错误之间存在相关性。需要理论模型来理解何时基于置信度的审计策略会失效，以及误校准的临界点在哪里。

Method: 将问题建模为两层高斯copula下的预算约束噪声检查（budgeted noisy inspection），形式化分析置信度排序审计（confidence-ranked auditing）与随机审计的误校准阈值δ*，并通过五个开源LLM与一个专有模型的实验验证理论预测，同时用记录轨迹回放策略进行验证。

Result: 发现两个反直觉结论：δ*随预算缩减而上升；跨家族相关性并不低，共享难度主导了谱系关系。五个开源LLM的置信度近似恒定（操作性无用），点估计处于或超过翻转点但置信区间跨越它；专有模型置信度信息丰富且低于阈值。给出了空洞监督（vacuous oversight）的量化判据。

Conclusion: 置信度排序审计并非总是优于随机审计，误校准达到阈值δ*后会翻转劣于随机。预算越小反而越需要更高的误校准阈值才能"翻转"，且跨模型家族的相关性主要由共享任务难度而非血缘关系决定。多数开源模型的置信度在实际操作中无用，而专有模型可为审计优先排序提供有效信息。

Abstract: A single human must audit $N$ LLM agents under a budget of $B \ll N$ audits per round, guided by self-reported confidence that may be adversarially miscalibrated and by correlated errors. We model this as budgeted noisy inspection over a two-level Gaussian copula and locate the miscalibration threshold $δ^*$ past which confidence-ranked auditing is \emph{worse} than random. Two a-priori expectations reverse: $δ^*$ \emph{rises} as the budget shrinks, and cross-family correlation is not low---shared difficulty dominates lineage. Five open-weight LLMs show operationally useless (near-constant) confidence, point estimates at or beyond the flip though CIs straddle it; a proprietary model is informative and lands below it. We give a quantitative criterion for \emph{vacuous} oversight, and replaying policies on recorded traces confirms the ordering.

</details>


### [112] [PathView-Bench: Can Multimodal Large Language Models Achieve Fine-grained Multiscale Understanding of Pathology Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28318)
*Zongyi Chen,Yu Liang,Jie Lin,Liansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathVU是一个视觉锚定的病理学多模态大语言模型基准测试，包含14个VQA任务、61,673张图像和308,070个样本，覆盖28个器官，用于评估模型在细粒度、多尺度病理图像上的视觉理解能力。评估18个代表性MLLM后发现即使是先进模型在细粒度视觉任务上仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理学多模态基准主要评估最终诊断答案、报告或描述，无法深入反映模型是否真正理解病理学推理所需的多尺度视觉内容。需要一个能够程序化评估细粒度、多尺度视觉理解的基准来弥补这一缺口。

Method: 从23个公开病理成像数据集构建PathVU，利用人工监督标签和空间标注，将原始标注转换为确定性任务目标，实现区域定位、视觉识别、数量估计、空间推理和上下文不足判断的程序化评分。基准涵盖Region FOV（高分辨率局部区域）和Slide FOV（宏观全玻片视图）两种视野，包含14个VQA任务。

Result: 在18个代表性通用、医学领域和病理专用MLLM上评估发现，即使是先进模型在跨多尺度病理图像的细粒度视觉任务上仍存在重大局限。

Conclusion: PathVU为开发和评估病理学MLLM提供了可复现的基础，以显式的多尺度视觉理解为导向，有助于推动模型在真实病理推理场景中的能力提升。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to analyze pathology images. However, dominant multimodal benchmarks in pathology mainly score final diagnostic answers, captions, or reports. These evaluations provide limited insight into whether a model understands the multiscale visual content needed for pathology reasoning and decision-making. We introduce PathVU, a vision-anchored benchmark for fine-grained and multiscale visual understanding in computational pathology. Built from 23 public pathology imaging datasets with human-supervised labels and spatial annotations, PathVU evaluates MLLM understanding in two fields of view: Region FOV for high-resolution local regions and Slide FOV for macro whole-slide views. By converting raw annotations into deterministic task targets, PathVU enables programmatic scoring of region localization, visual recognition, quantity estimation, spatial reasoning, and insufficient-context judgment. The benchmark contains 14 VQA-style tasks, 61,673 images, and 308,070 samples across 28 organs and 7,253,526 annotations. Evaluating 18 representative general-purpose, medical-domain, and pathology-oriented MLLMs, we observe substantial limitations even in advanced models on fine-grained visual tasks across multiscale pathology images. PathVU provides a reproducible basis for developing and evaluating pathology MLLMs with explicit multiscale visual understanding.

</details>


### [113] [Paying for Honesty Without Knowing the Truth: Reputation-Penalty Design for LLM Marketplace Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28330)
*Mingdai Yang,Shicheng Fan,Kejing Yu,Duohao Wang,Li Sun,Hao Peng,Philip S. Yu,Zhiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARP机制，一种基于声誉惩罚的机制，通过容忍投诉噪声的"死区"和状态依赖的惩罚强度来抑制LLM商家在商品列表中虚构属性。CARP无需产品级真实信息，与SPARC机制（字节级代码门控反思机制）结合后，可显著缩小相对于完美信息基线的消费者福利差距，并行为上约束LLM商家不进行虚构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理正越来越多地作为自主商家撰写商品列表，在竞争压力下，即使在明确指示要诚实的条件下，它们也会在大多数列表中虚构属性。平台无法逐一验证每项声明的真实性，因为平台只能观察到含噪且有偏的投诉信号，而无法获得真实标签，因此需要一种无需真实信息即可抑制虚构行为的机制。

Method: 设计CARP（声誉惩罚机制），包含两个关键设计：(1)死区（deadband）用于容忍投诉中的噪声；(2)状态依赖的惩罚强度，用于对抗声誉驱动的检测侵蚀。同时设计SPARC（字节级代码门控反思机制），使惩罚在行为上可约束LLM商家。CARP无需产品级真实信息，对策略性博弈具有鲁棒性。

Result: CARP通过压制低评分说谎者的销售规模来保护消费者，同时不伤害诚实卖家。与SPARC配对后，相较于完美信息预言机，可以弥合大部分消费者福利差距，在比较的所有策略中实现最优福利。LLM商家在谎言无成本时会虚构，但当虚构导致销售损失时会自我约束——这是自利响应而非合规，且该效应在多个模型上一致观测到，有置信区间支持。

Conclusion: LLM商家的诚实行为并非源于道德合规，而是源于自利激励——只有当虚构会影响其销售时，它们才会停止虚构。CARP+SPARC机制通过市场反馈而非显性监督来实现行为约束，为平台治理LLM生成内容中的不实陈述提供了一种无需访问真实信息的可行方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act as autonomous merchants that write their own product listings, and under competitive pressure, they fabricate attributes to win sales. Even under instructions to be honest, they fabricate attributes in a majority of listings across models. A platform's obvious remedy---verifying each claim against the truth---is unavailable, because it observes only a noisy, biased complaint signal, never the ground truth. We design CARP, a reputation-penalty mechanism with a deadband that forgives complaint noise and a state-dependent severity that counters reputation-driven detection erosion. CARP requires no product-level ground truth and is robust to strategic gaming. CARP protects consumers by suppressing the sales volume of low-rated liars while sparing honest sellers. Paired with SPARC, it closes most of the consumer-welfare gap relative to a perfect-information oracle, without ever accessing the truth. It also achieves the best welfare of the policies we compare. We further show that this felt penalty becomes behaviorally binding through SPARC, a byte-clean code-gated reflection mechanism: LLM merchants fabricate when lying is free but restrain themselves when fabrication costs them sales, a self-interested response rather than compliance. We trace this distinction to penalty-gated self-correction reasoning, and observe the binding across models, with supporting confidence intervals.

</details>


### [114] [Correcting What You Cannot See: Credit Assignment for Perception Distillation in Multimodal Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28336)
*Feng Xiong,Leyan Xue,Hongyu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出感知修正蒸馏（PCD），一种无标签方法，通过下游失败与师生分歧两个互补证据的乘积（软AND门）来识别可纠正的感知失败，从而在多模态推理蒸馏中提供更强的密集监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏为多模态推理器提供密集监督，但其轨迹级奖励无法区分失败源于感知还是推理。现有的感知成功率（PSR）估计存在歧义——低成功既可能是感知不足也可能是推理困难，导致无法准确识别需要修正的感知失败。

Method: PCD方法包含三个关键组成部分：（1）利用下游失败和师生分歧作为互补的"见证者"，通过贝叶斯证据组合推导出乘积规则作为理想的软AND门——乘法是唯一的归一化双线性门，且当任一见证缺失时归零；（2）采用分离的感知-推理回滚机制；（3）使用均值保持权重，确保推理目标函数不受影响。

Result: 在8个基准上，PCD将8B→2B的宏平均从44.50（OPD）提升至47.28，32B→8B从56.94提升至61.22。在匹配的2B消融实验中，移除PCD和分离回滚分别导致平均分数下降2.22和0.88分。

Conclusion: 有效多模态蒸馏不仅取决于教师预测什么，还取决于识别何时感知才是正确的修正目标。PCD通过同时利用下游失败与师生分歧信号，显著优于传统同策略蒸馏方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multimodal reasoners, but its trajectory-level reward cannot determine whether a failed answer arose from perception or subsequent reasoning. Perception Success Rate (PSR), estimated from multiple reasonings sharing one perception, remains ambiguous because low success conflates perceptual insufficiency with reasoning difficulty. We introduce \textbf{Perception-Correction Distillation (PCD)}, a label-free method that identifies correctable perception failures using downstream failure and teacher--student disagreement as complementary witnesses. Their product, , forms a soft AND gate that strengthens distillation only when both witnesses are present. We motivate this rule through Bayesian evidence combination and show that multiplication is the unique normalized bilinear gate that vanishes when either witness is absent. PCD uses separated perception--reasoning rollouts and mean-preserving weights, leaving the reasoning objective unchanged. Across eight benchmarks, PCD improves the 8B 2B macro average from 44.50 with OPD to 47.28 and the 32B 8B result from 56.94 to 61.22. In matched 2B ablations, removing PCD and separated rollout reduces held-out average by 2.22 and 0.88 points, respectively. Effective multimodal distillation therefore depends not only on what the teacher predicts, but also on identifying when perception is the appropriate target of correction.

</details>


### [115] [How Benchmarks Mis-Score Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28367)
*Zihan Dong,Zhiyuan Ma,Zekun Wang,Yunqing Li,Zirou Liu,Ruixuan Deng,Qishi Zhan,Rui Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对计算机使用智能体（CUA）的基准评估可靠性进行了系统性审计，发现15.3%的失败判定是错误的（10.7%为评估器误报，4.7%为任务缺陷），并提出了覆盖任务构建、轨迹观测、评分和报告四阶段的可靠性框架及三层次诊断分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA智能体正被部署用于网页浏览和桌面软件操作，但其基准分数仍由脆弱的脚本化预言机产生。现有评估管线中任务过时、轨迹缺少关键视觉证据、评估器拒绝合法备选方案、聚合报告掩盖失败原因等问题严重威胁评估可信度，亟需系统性梳理与量化审计。

Method: 作者首先构建了一个覆盖任务构建、轨迹观测、评分和报告四个阶段的可靠性框架；随后审计了来自5个网页、企业工作流和桌面控制基准的150条公开失败评分轨迹，量化错误判定比例；最后提出了三层次诊断分类法来区分真实失败的原因类型。

Result: 审计发现15.3%的FAIL判定是错误的，其中10.7%为评估器假阴性、4.7%为任务本身损坏。对于真实失败，验证/反馈错误和规划错误占主导，而非执行/接地错误，单一标量成功率无法解释这些差异。研究还将发现与更新的长视界CUA基准联系起来，得出了各阶段的评估设计规则。

Conclusion: CUA评估的可靠性问题系统性存在于从任务构建到报告的全流程中，现有单一成功率指标不足以刻画失败模式，需要针对不同评估阶段制定专门的设计规则来提升CUA基准评估的可信度。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUA) are being deployed to browse the web and operate desktop software, yet their benchmark scores are still commonly produced by brittle scripted oracles. A score is the output of a pipeline in which tasks can be stale, trajectories can omit decisive visual evidence, evaluators can reject valid alternatives, and aggregate reports can hide the cause of failure. We organize these problems into a reliability framework spanning task construction, trajectory observation, scoring, and reporting. We then audit 150 public failure-scored trajectories from five web, enterprise-workflow, and desktop-control benchmarks, find that 15.3\% of FAIL verdicts are wrong: 10.7\% are evaluator false negatives and 4.7\% are broken tasks. For genuine failures, a three-tier diagnostic taxonomy shows that verification/feedback and planning failures dominate execution/grounding errors, while a single scalar success rate can not explain. We connect these findings to newer long-horizon CUA benchmarks and derive stage-specific design rules for CUA evaluation.

</details>


### [116] [HyperClaim: Fine-Grained Cross-Modal Hypergraph Reasoning for Video Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28375)
*Xiangbo Wang,Jiasheng Zhang,Xingtong Yu,Luoqiang Lei,Delvin Ce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HyperClaim，一个基于判别式时间超图框架的样本级视频虚假信息真实性分类方法。通过构建稀疏异质超图，捕捉查询词、上下文文本和短时间帧片段之间多路跨模态高阶依赖，在FactGuard时间协议下于FakeSV、FakeTT和FakeVV三个数据集上分别达到83.7%、82.0%和87.3%的准确率，优于强判别式和推理式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚假信息检测方法主要依赖全局多模态融合或自由形式多模态推理，二者都可能低估由查询短语、上下文文本与短时间帧片段耦合交互产生的局部真实性线索。这类交互本质上是高阶的，成对图结构无法充分建模多路跨模态依赖，而超图能更合适地表达这些关系。

Method: HyperClaim包含五个核心组件：1) 以标题或基准提供的配对文本作为声明式查询，在查询词元、证据词元和采样帧上构建稀疏异质超图；2) 应用置信度感知过滤和来源预算，形成紧凑的文本-帧和短程时间证据单元；3) 执行自适应软关联推理并辅以残差文本-视频校准；4) 通过差异感知读取聚合文本、视觉和超边状态；5) 无需生成推理链或外部工具调用，保留被全局融合压平的细粒度跨模态与时间结构。

Result: 在FactGuard时间协议下，HyperClaim在FakeSV、FakeTT和FakeVV上分别取得83.7%、82.0%和87.3%的准确率，优于强判别式基线和推理式基线。学习到的关联权重与注意力权重还揭示了词元级和帧级结构。

Conclusion: HyperClaim验证了超图表示在视频虚假信息检测中的有效性，能够在不依赖生成式推理链或外部工具的情况下，捕捉高阶跨模态与时间结构，为细粒度真实性检测提供了新的判别式框架方向。

Abstract: Video misinformation detection is often approached through global multimodal fusion or free-form multimodal reasoning. Both paradigms can under-represent localized authenticity cues that arise from coupled interactions among query phrases, contextual text, and short temporal spans of frames. Because such interactions are inherently higher-order, pairwise graph formulations are insufficient to capture multi-way cross-modal dependencies, whereas hypergraphs offer a suitable representation for these relations. We propose HyperClaim, a discriminative temporal hypergraph framework for sample-level authenticity classification. Using the title or benchmark-provided paired text as a claim-like query, HyperClaim constructs a sparse heterogeneous hypergraph over query tokens, evidence tokens, and sampled frames; applies confidence-aware filtering and source budgeting to form compact text-frame and short-range temporal evidence units; performs adaptive soft-incidence reasoning with residual text-video calibration; and aggregates textual, visual, and hyperedge states through a discrepancy-aware readout. Without relying on generated rationales or external tool calls, HyperClaim preserves fine-grained cross-modal and temporal structure that global fusion tends to flatten. Under the FactGuard temporal protocol, it achieves 83.7%, 82.0%, and 87.3% accuracy on FakeSV, FakeTT, and FakeVV, respectively, outperforming strong discriminative and reasoning-centric baselines. Learned incidence and attention weights further reveal token- and frame-level structure.

</details>


### [117] [When Specifications Conflict: A Symmetry-Based Framework for Measuring LLM Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28384)
*Tairan Wang,Liang Zhou,Zikang Zhan,Pingchuan Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可控实验框架，用于研究大语言模型在面临不一致/冲突的规范说明时的偏好选择。通过对550个冲突实例（涵盖11个函数族、4种表示类型）的评估，发现模型表现出系统性的偏好模式而非随机行为，偏好排序为：形式语言≈自然化的形式语言>纯自然语言>输入-输出示例。该框架还扩展到布尔代数、代码生成和临床领域，展示了跨任务和规范形式的广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地需要整合可能不一致或冲突的多种信息来源，但目前缺乏可控且可归因的方法来分析模型如何解决竞争性规范之间的冲突。

Method: 提出一个受控实验框架：通过构造具有显式冲突的规范来直接观察和分析模型在竞争性规范之间的选择；采用基于对称性的设计减少混淆因素，使跨表示类型的偏好能够被系统比较。在含550个冲突实例、11个函数族的可执行数学基准上进行评估，并扩展到异构规范冲突场景。

Result: 模型表现出系统性的偏好模式（非随机），偏好排序一致为：形式语言≈自然化的形式语言>纯自然语言>输入-输出示例。示例效应依赖于模型能力和函数族。框架在布尔代数、代码生成和临床领域均展现适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种统一的方法来衡量LLM如何解决竞争性信息来源之间的冲突，适用于多样化的任务和规范形式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly required to integrate multiple sources of information that may be inconsistent or conflicting. However, there is still a lack of controllable and attributable methods for analyzing how models resolve conflicts between competing specifications. We propose a controlled experimental framework for studying model preferences under conflicting specifications. By constructing specifications with explicit conflicts, the framework enables model choices between competing specifications to be directly observed and analyzed. A symmetry-based design further reduces confounding factors, allowing preferences across representation types to be compared systematically.
  We evaluate the framework on an executable mathematical benchmark with 550 conflict instances spanning 11 function families, comparing four representation types: pure natural language, formal language, naturalized formal language, and input--output examples. Results show systematic preference patterns rather than random behavior, with a consistent ordering: $ \text{Formal} \approx \text{Naturalized Formal} > \text{Pure Natural Language} > \text{Input--Output Examples} $. Example effects further depend on model capability and function family.
  We extend the framework to heterogeneous specification conflicts in Boolean algebra, code generation, and the clinical domain, demonstrating its applicability across diverse tasks and specification forms. The framework provides a unified approach for measuring how LLMs resolve conflicts between competing sources of information.

</details>


### [118] [QuantWAMs: Calibrating at the Right Granularity for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28405)
*Jiacheng Zhou,Jinfan Lv,Ruixuan Li,Longtai Zhang,Yan Wang,Wenqiang Zhang,Lizhe Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了QuantWAMs，一个专为世界动作模型（WAMs）设计的后训练量化（PTQ）框架，通过三种策略（共享基异常值校准、协同训练目标显著性、固定干预回滚审计）将量化决策与模型结构、回滚分布和任务目标对齐，在W4A4主导设置下仿真均值与FP16仅差0.2-0.7个百分点，峰值权重量化内存降至FP16的约29%，并提供1.4-1.6倍的模块级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）联合预测未来观测和动作，但其迭代去噪和闭环执行导致部署成本高昂。现有PTQ方法不适合WAMs，因为它们依赖开环目标、同质模型假设，且校准分布不能反映实际部署场景。

Method: QuantWAMs包含三个关键策略：(1) 共享基异常值校准——仅在坐标兼容模块间池化激活证据；(2) 协同训练目标显著性——从联合视频-动作梯度计算经验Fisher分数，并在校准稳定的层粒度上分配权重精度；(3) 固定干预回滚审计——利用可达闭环状态修订去噪步保护调度而不改变精度预算。

Result: 在Fast-WAM和LingBot-VA上，覆盖RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及AgiBot G2真实机器人操作。W4A4主导设置下仿真均值与FP16相差仅0.2-0.7个百分点。真实机器人试验验证了三个操作任务的部署可行性。视频和动作块的峰值权重-激活内存降至FP16的约29%，提供1.4-1.6倍模块级加速。

Conclusion: QuantWAMs通过将量化决策与WAMs的实际部署上下文（模型结构、回滚分布和任务目标）对齐，有效解决了传统PTQ方法在WAMs上的适配不足问题，在不改变精度预算的前提下显著降低内存占用并提升推理速度，同时保持与FP16接近的性能。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, but their iterative denoising and closed-loop execution make efficient deployment costly. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods are poorly suited to WAMs because they rely on open-loop objectives, homogeneous model assumptions, and calibration distributions that do not reflect deployment. We present QuantWAMs, a PTQ framework that aligns quantization decisions with the calibration context defined by model structure, rollout distribution, and task objective. QuantWAMs introduces three strategies: shared-basis outlier calibration, which pools activation evidence only across coordinate-compatible modules; co-training-objective saliency, which computes empirical-Fisher scores from the joint video--action gradient and assigns weight precision at a calibration-stable layer granularity; and fixed-intervention rollout auditing, which revises denoising-step protection schedules using reachable closed-loop states without changing the precision budget. We evaluate QuantWAMs on Fast-WAM and LingBot-VA across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and real-robot manipulation with an AgiBot G2. Under a W4A4-dominant setting, the reported simulation means differ from FP16 by 0.2--0.7 percentage points. Real-robot trials further establish deployment feasibility on three manipulation tasks. For the targeted video and action blocks, QuantWAMs reduces peak weight-and-activation memory to about 29\% of FP16 and provides 1.4--1.6$\times$ block-level speedups.

</details>


### [119] [WIDE: Boosting Adaptive LLM Inference via Token-level Dynamic Width Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28418)
*Haozhe Hu,Hao Wu,Peiran Yin,Chao Han,Yunpu Ma,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WIDE是首个面向LLM预填充和解码场景的端到端可微分token级动态宽度剪枝框架，允许每个token动态选择注意力头组和FFN通道组，将动态剪枝从层级别扩展到神经元块级别。通过两阶段训练和剪枝-内核协同设计，在50%稀疏度下相比最先进的动态深度剪枝提升55.1%性能，解码端理论核级加速最高4.95倍，端到端加速1.55倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态结构化剪枝方法硬件友好且有实际吞吐量收益，但输入无关的计算分配在激进稀疏度下造成严重精度退化；现有的动态稀疏方法虽然通过适配单个输入提升了质量保持能力，但大多局限于粗粒度的结构决策，实际推理场景下的加速效果仍具挑战。

Method: WIDE框架包含三个核心组件：(1) token级动态宽度剪枝，允许每个token独立选择注意力头组和FFN通道组，实现神经元块级别的细粒度计算分配；(2) 两阶段训练流水线，学习有效的token级稀疏执行模式；(3) 剪枝-内核协同设计框架，将动态稀疏加速分解为掩码重排序（mask reordering）、硬件无关的块级跳过和硬件相关的块内跳过三个层次，以支持不同粒度的高效执行。

Result: 在50%稀疏度下，WIDE相比最先进的动态深度剪枝在仅校准（calibration-only）设置下提供55.1%的性能提升。在预填充和解码推理工作负载下，WIDE分别达到最高1.98倍和4.95倍的接近理论极限的核级加速，以及1.68倍和1.55倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: WIDE证明了将动态剪枝从层级别推进到神经元块级别的可行性，并通过剪枝-内核协同设计使得细粒度动态稀疏在实际推理中实现接近理论极限的加速，同时保持出色的模型质量。

Abstract: Pruning is a promising approach for improving the efficiency of LLMs. Existing static structured pruning methods are hardware-friendly and can deliver practical throughput gains, but their input-agnostic computation allocation often causes substantial accuracy degradation under aggressive sparsity. Recent dynamic sparsity methods improve quality retention by adapting computation to individual inputs, yet they remain largely limited to coarse-grained structural decisions and their practical acceleration under real-world inference scenarios remains challenging. To address these challenges, we present WIDE, the first end-to-end differentiable token-level dynamic width pruning framework designed for both prefill and decode scenarios. WIDE enables fine-grained computation allocation by allowing each token to dynamically select attention-head groups and FFN-channel groups, extending dynamic pruning beyond layer-level decisions to neuron-block-level granularity. Through a two-stage training pipeline, WIDE learns effective token-wise sparse execution patterns and achieves substantially better quality retention than existing approaches. To make such fine-grained dynamic pruning practical, we further propose a pruning--kernel co-design framework that decomposes dynamic sparsity acceleration into mask reordering, hardware-agnostic block-level skipping, and hardware-dependent intra-block skipping, enabling efficient execution across different granularities. At 50% sparsity, WIDE provides 55.1% performance boost when compared to the state-of-the-art dynamic depth pruning under calibration-only settings. Under prefill and decoding inference workloads, WIDE achieves close-to-theoretical kernel-level speedups of up to 1.98x for prefill and 4.95x for decoding, as well as 1.68x and 1.55x end-to-end acceleration. Our code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning/tree/main/WIDE.

</details>


### [120] [When Derived Measurements Mislead: Quantifying and Mitigating LLM Over-Trust with Privileged-Modality Reliability Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28421)
*Zongheng Guo,Tao Chen,Tianli Li,Mingzhe Cui,Yang Jiao,Lei Xie,Yi Pan,Xiao Hu,Manuela Ferrario*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了"派生特征过度信任（DFOT）"问题：LLM将来自派生测量的数据误当作直接事实或超出其有效范围使用。以生理感知为例，设计了两个测试（D1/D2）和五个量化指标（COTR、CIR、CRR、ESRM、UHR），并在50,000条配对PPG-ECG记录上验证了ECG-to-PPG特权蒸馏基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）管道中，越来越多的派生测量结果被当作直接事实使用，但这些测量的有效性依赖于具体实例。这种过度信任可能导致错误决策，目前缺乏一个系统的评估框架来量化和比较不同缓解方法的效果。

Method: 提出了DFOT（派生特征过度信任）形式化定义，包含两个针对性测试：D1检测与离线ECG相矛盾的PPG派生心律被接受的情况，D2检测在误导性严重病史下离线确认可靠的PPG心律被拒绝的情况。设计了五个估算量（COTR、CIR、CRR、ESRM、UHR）来量化链条中的不同失败和修复维度。框架不依赖特定可靠性生成器，在50,000条配对PPG-ECG记录上以ECG-to-PPG特权蒸馏作为基线进行了演示。

Result: 在协议锁定的187名患者测试集上，基线在四个修复和特异性终点上提高了1.82-6.69个百分点，所有配对置信区间均排除零；UHR增加0.67个百分点（95% CI: -0.4至+1.7）。

Conclusion: DFOT提供了一个通用的评估目标，用于比较和推动更强的派生测量误用缓解方法的发展。代码已开源。

Abstract: Derived measurements increasingly enter large language model (LLM) pipelines as direct facts despite their instance-dependent validity. We define derived-feature over-trust (DFOT) as the failure in which a downstream LLM assigns such a measurement the epistemic status of a direct fact or uses it outside its valid scope. Using physiological sensing as a case study, D1 tests acceptance of a PPG-derived rhythm contradicted by offline ECG, whereas D2 tests rejection of an offline-confirmed reliable PPG rhythm under misleading severe history. ECG supplies training supervision and offline reference construction but is never shown to the LLM. Five estimands quantify this chain: conflict over-trust rate (COTR) and context-induced error rate (CIR) characterize D1/D2; correct repair rate (CRR) measures frozen-error repair; evidence-specific repair margin (ESRM) contrasts matched and patient-disjoint shuffled evidence; and utility harm rate (UHR) measures unnecessary verification among HIGH-reliability cases used without verification at baseline. The framework does not depend on a particular reliability generator. We demonstrate it on 50,000 paired PPG-ECG records using ECG-to-PPG privileged distillation as an illustrative baseline and PPG-only inference. On a protocol-locked 187-patient test, the baseline improves four repair and specificity endpoints by 1.82-6.69 percentage points, with all paired confidence intervals excluding zero; UHR increases by 0.67 percentage points (95% CI: -0.4 to +1.7). DFOT provides a common evaluation target for stronger mitigation methods. The code is available at https://github.com/Zongheng-Guo/When-Derived-Measurements-Mislead.

</details>


### [121] [A foundation model of numerical intelligence with cross-disciplinary generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28432)
*Chenghan Wu,Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UNICON（UNified In-Context Operator Networks），一个在数值领域展现"数值智能"的基础模型。通过图结构示例作为上下文，UNICON能够跨学科推断预测关系并应用于新查询，即使面对训练中未出现的学科也能逼近专家性能；与语言模型智能体结合后甚至可超越专家系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能研究多聚焦于语言模型，但科学与社会系统中的知识获取与应用往往涉及数值数据而非文本。现有模型缺乏从数值上下文中推断和泛化预测关系的能力，因此需要一种在数值领域具备类似语言模型"上下文学习"能力的通用基础模型。

Method: UNICON采用图结构示例作为上下文输入，从同一系统中的多个示例中推断共享的预测关系，并将其应用于同系统的查询。训练语料覆盖多学科数据，并在评估中纳入训练中未见的学科以测试跨学科泛化能力。此外将UNICON与语言模型智能体结合，实现更高级的推理与预测。

Result: UNICON在多个科学与社会系统中，包括训练中缺失的学科，无需重训练即可逼近专家级性能；与语言模型智能体集成后，在训练中未见学科上超越最先进专家模型；训练语料多样性越强，对未见学科的泛化能力越好。

Conclusion: UNICON作为数值智能的基础模型，为跨学科数值预测任务提供了通用的零样本/少样本解决方案，并可作为更广泛人工智能生态系统的构建模块。

Abstract: Intelligence is commonly understood as the ability to acquire and apply knowledge, adapt to unfamiliar situations and solve new problems. Large language models exhibit this capacity by inferring task-relevant knowledge from textual context and applying it to new tasks. Yet intelligence need not be confined to language. For scientific and social systems, we need models that acquire and apply knowledge from numerical context-an ability we call numerical intelligence. Here we introduce UNified In-Context Operator Networks (UNICON), a foundation model that exhibits numerical intelligence across disciplines. Using graph-based examples from a system as context, UNICON infers the predictive relation shared across them and applies it to queries from the same system. Across scientific and social systems, including those from disciplines absent from training, the same model approaches specialist performance without retraining. Combining UNICON with language-model agents yields further gains, enabling it to surpass state-of-the-art specialists in a discipline unseen in training. We further show that training-corpus diversity improves generalization to unseen disciplines. Together, these results establish UNICON as a foundation model of numerical intelligence and position it as a building block for a broader ecosystem of artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [122] [Metaphor Tracer: A Theory-Informed Analysis of Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28434)
*Marc Heimann,Roxana Assadi Moghaddam,Olga Brovkina,Mark Pettifor,Lutz Goetzmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种无需训练的隐藏状态评分方法——聚合器（aggregator）和分化器（differentiator），前者衡量某一位置是否将整篇文本整合为稳定配置，后者衡量其他标记是否被瞬时带入其子空间（即"隐喻"的运输本义）。在多个无关语言模型中，聚合器分数不随重复标记的惊奇度与注意力下降而改变，标记的是该标记在文本中的位置。通过6/6和34/36的独立真值验证（工程注册、精神分析师的临床转录标记），并结合迁移测试，证明该分数反映的是标记在特定文本中的关系性结构价值，而非其向量本身的本质属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探索语言模型隐藏状态能否揭示单篇文本的组织方式，并测试一种"关系性"而非"本质主义"的隐藏状态解读——即标记的意义来自其在文本中的位置关系，而非其向量本身。

Method: 仅通过一次前向传播，无需训练，对每个标记位置计算两个分数：聚合器（衡量该位置是否将全文整合为稳定配置）和分化器（衡量其他标记是否被瞬时携带进入其子空间）。通过控制变量（在一个发现文本上冻结常量，其余文本均为确认性实验），并借助独立真值（工程注册、精神分析师事先标记的临床转录）及跨模型、跨类型迁移测试进行验证。

Result: 聚合器并非经典信息度量或显著性度量；在三个无关模型中，即使重复标记的惊奇度和注意力下降，聚合器分数仍保持稳定。独立真值验证达到6/6单元和34/36单元的一致率，并有逐级增量高于词汇对照。迁移测试显示：标记结构随词汇类型迁移的模型对单篇话语的阅读效果最差；在匹配的基础/指令对中，微调提高保真度但未改变类型迁移。

Conclusion: 结构价值是标记在特定文本中位置的属性，而非其向量本身的属性。这为隐藏状态提供了关系性而非本质主义的解读，并将先于仪器存在的理论进行了操作性实现。

Abstract: What do a language model's hidden states say about the organization of a single text? From one forward pass, without training, we score every token position on two properties. The *aggregator* measures whether the position consolidates the whole text into a stable configuration. The *differentiator*, whether other tokens are transiently carried into its subspace as the model reads: metaphor in its root sense, transport. Constants were frozen on one discovery text; every other is confirmatory.
  The aggregator is not, in the classic sense, an information measure, nor a measure of salience. Across three unrelated models, as a signifier repeats, its surprisal and its attention drain while its aggregator score holds: the channel marks a token's place in the text. That this tracks a reading rests on independent ground truth: an engineered register the aggregator follows across its boundaries (6/6 cells), and a psychoanalyst's marking of clinical transcripts, fixed before the instrument existed, in 34/36 cells, with a graded increment above lexical controls and dissociations no type-level measure reproduces. A transfer test gives the result its shape: the model whose token structure travels with lexical type reads the singular discourse worst, and in a matched base/instruct pair tuning raises fidelity without moving type-transfer. Structural value is a property of a token's place in *this* text, not of its vector alone: a relational rather than essentialist reading of hidden states, operationalizing theory that predated the instrument.

</details>


### [123] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR)，一个无外部反馈的多轮强化学习框架，通过自我验证作为计算控制策略，在保证高精度的同时显著减少推理轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时计算扩展方法要么使用统一的计算预算（在简单输入上浪费计算），要么依赖验证器引导的细化（需要外部反馈）。作者希望学习一种无需oracle的内部自验证信号，来自适应地控制何时停止细化、何时继续推理。

Method: SVR在每一轮生成解决方案时附带离散正确性判定和置信度分数；仅当判定为"正确"且置信度超过阈值时才保留当前答案，否则继续自我验证驱动的细化。真实标签仅用于构建训练奖励（GRPO），推理时完全不需要。训练使用固定水平轨迹上的GRPO，奖励同时鼓励解题正确性、校准感知的自验证和可停止的正确状态；自适应停止仅在推理时激活。

Result: 在7个数学推理基准上，基于Qwen3.5-2B的SVR实现了0.563的宏平均准确率，平均仅需2.99个推理轮次。在完整系统对比中，超越了标准GRPO、强多轮基线和固定预算的oracle引导分数反馈基线，且所需推理轮次远少于固定10轮推理。

Conclusion: 学习到的自我验证可以作为有效的内部控制信号，用于答案保留和自适应测试时计算分配，在无需外部反馈的情况下实现计算效率与推理精度的良好平衡。

Abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>


### [124] [LeanCSP: A Framework for Certifying Constraint Reformulation and Solving in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28459)
*Pablo Manrique,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在Lean定理证明器中引入了一个形式化验证框架，同时解决约束编程中两个层级的信任问题：公式重构的语义保持性（如等价性、等满足性和对称性破缺约束的正确性），以及外部求解器输出证书的正确性。该框架支持对问题族进行参数化证明，并通过翻译后端（MiniZinc、SMT-LIB、OPB）检查单个实例的求解证书，实现端到端的可信工作流。实验显示，参数化对称性破缺证明可将搜索开销降低高达2×10^7倍，且证书验证在几分钟内完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束编程是解决调度、规划、配置和验证中复杂组合问题的核心技术，但其结果的可信度需要两个层面的保证：一是前置重构（reformulations）必须保持语义，二是求解器必须产生正确的答案。现有方法缺乏一个统一的框架同时保证这两个层面。

Method: 在Lean定理证明器中构建了一个双层级验证框架：1）公式层面：参数化地证明问题族的等价性、等满足性及对称性破缺约束的正确性；2）实例层面：通过翻译后端（MiniZinc、SMT-LIB、OPB）将问题翻译为外部格式，并检查求解器返回的证书。

Result: 实验表明，每个问题族只需一次参数化证明，可跨所有实例规模复用，将求解器搜索开销降低最多达2×10^7倍；整个在Lean内部的证书验证过程保持在可接受开销范围内，最大实例也只需几分钟。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了端到端的验证工作流，无需信任外部求解器即可建立约束问题的可满足性或不可满足性，同时兼顾了理论保证和实际效率，弥合了约束编程理论与实践验证之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Constraint programming is a core technology for solving complex combinatorial problems in scheduling, planning, configuration, and verification. Trusting its results therefore demands guarantees at two levels: that reformulations applied beforehand are semantics-preserving, and that solvers produce correct answers. In this work, we introduce a framework that addresses both verification levels in the Lean theorem prover: it can be used to prove formulation-level properties, such as equivalence, equisatisfiability, and the correctness of symmetry-breaking constraints, parametrically for entire problem families; and to check solver-produced certificates for individual instances via translation backends to external formats such as MiniZinc, SMT-LIB, and OPB. Combining both levels yields an end-to-end workflow that establishes the satisfiability or unsatisfiability of a constraint problem without trusting the external solver. Experimental results show that our framework's verified symmetry breaking also pays off in practice: a single parametric proof per problem family, reused across all instance sizes, reduces solver search effort by a factor of up to 2x10^7, while the entire in-Lean certification stays affordable, taking at most a few minutes for our largest instances.

</details>


### [125] [A report-grounded vision-language foundation model for colonoscopy from 280000 routine reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28466)
*Jia Yu,Yan Zhu,Yili He,Zilong Wang,Xinyang Jiang,Peiyao Fu,Ruijie Yang,Tianyi Chen,Siyuan Li,Zhihua Wang,Fei Wu,Quanlin Li,Xian Yang,Pinghong Zhou,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoCLIP，一种基于结肠镜检查的视觉-语言基础模型，从280,476份常规结肠镜报告中逐步恢复出125,756个病灶级图像-文本对进行训练。在病灶级图像-文本检索、结构化报告生成和六个多中心临床分类任务中，EndoCLIP在零样本和线性探针设置下均优于通用及生物医学视觉-语言编码器，其良恶性分类线性探针性能接近12名内镜专家盲读结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜报告中蕴含丰富的专家描述（病灶外观、大小、位置），但这些报告是整段手术过程的摘要而非逐帧标注，导致临床发现与对应图像之间缺乏强关联。视觉-语言模型在结肠镜领域因此未被充分利用，且每个临床任务通常需要单独标注数据，成本高昂。

Method: 从280,476份常规结肠镜报告中进行渐进式（progressive）恢复，构建125,756个病灶级图像-文本训练对；基于对比学习（类似CLIP架构）训练EndoCLIP视觉-语言基础模型；在多种任务中采用零样本和线性探针两种评估方式。

Result: 在病灶级图像-文本检索、结构化报告生成和六个多中心临床分类任务上，EndoCLIP均优于通用和生物医学视觉-语言编码器。在良恶性分类中，其线性探针性能接近12名内镜专家参与的盲读研究中的专家水平。

Conclusion: 将常规文档中的临床发现恢复为与图像帧的对应关系，可以将日常记录转化为可扩展的监督信号，使临床目标能够用自然语言来定义，而无需为每个任务单独进行标注。

Abstract: Vision-language models remain underused in colonoscopy despite the rich expert descriptions recorded in routine reports. These reports document lesion appearance, size and location but summarise entire procedures rather than caption individual frames, leaving clinical findings only weakly linked to the corresponding images. Here we develop EndoCLIP, a colonoscopy vision-language foundation model trained on 125,756 lesion-level image-text pairs progressively recovered from 280,476 routine colonoscopy records. Across lesion-level image-text retrieval, structured report generation and six multi-centre clinical classification tasks, EndoCLIP outperforms general-purpose and biomedical vision-language encoders in both zero-shot and linear-probe settings. On benign-versus-malignant classification, its linear probe approaches the performance of expert readers in a blinded study involving 12 endoscopists. These results suggest that recovering finding-to-frame correspondence can transform routine documentation into scalable supervision, enabling clinical targets to be specified in language rather than separately annotated for each task.

</details>


### [126] [Towards Autonomous Aircraft Surveillance from Nanosatellites through On-Board Inference and Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28470)
*Antonio Delgado-Rosa,David Muñoz-Valero,Enrique Adrian Villarrubia-Martin,Juan Moreno-Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合星上推理与生成式数据增强的工作流，用于克服纳米卫星对地监视中的下行链路瓶颈和开放航空数据集稀少且类别不平衡的问题。在6U立方星上部署低功耗边缘张量加速器执行推理，同时用低秩微调的扩散模型生成合成少数类图像并自动伪标注，与经典增强样本合并训练检测器。实验表明平衡数据集使全局mAP从77.9%提升至82.2%，少数类F1从0.683提升至0.811，量化检测器适配星上内存并可实现在轨25-30帧/秒处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前低地球轨道机载监视面临两个相互关联的瓶颈：一是纳米卫星下行链路预算有限，但传统方法仍需将数TB原始影像传输至地面处理，导致决策延迟；二是面向飞机的开放卫星数据集稀缺且类别严重不平衡，阻碍标准检测器学习罕见飞机类别的鲁棒表征。因此需要一种能同时在轨端处理数据并缓解数据不平衡的联合解决方案。

Method: 提出结合星上推理与生成式数据增强的两阶段工作流：在6U立方星上部署低功耗边缘张量加速器执行实时目标检测推理；地面端使用经低秩适应（LoRA）微调的扩散模型生成合成少数类航空影像，由中间检测器自动生成伪标签，并与经典数据增强样本合并构成平衡数据集，用于训练检测器。

Result: 平衡数据集使全局平均精度（mAP）从77.9%提升至82.2%，少数类F1分数从0.683提升至0.811；经过量化后的检测器适配星上芯片内存，可实现在轨25-30帧/秒的推理速度。

Conclusion: 计算实验验证了所提工作流可作为纳米卫星实时自主机载监视的决策支持工具。相比传统的弯曲管道（bent-pipe）架构——卫星仅作为被动数据收集器——本文方法将卫星转变为具备自主决策能力的智能节点，同时解决了下行链路带宽限制与数据类别不平衡两大瓶颈。

Abstract: Airborne surveillance from low Earth orbit is hindered by two interconnected bottlenecks: nanosatellites have a limited downlink budget, yet the conventional approach still transmits terabytes of raw imagery to the ground for processing, and open satellite datasets for aircraft are scarce and severely class-imbalanced. These limitations either delay timely decision-making or prevent standard detectors from learning robust representations of rare aircraft classes. In this paper, a workflow that combines on-board inference with generative data augmentation is proposed to address both limitations jointly. Inference is executed on a 6U CubeSat equipped with a low-power edge tensor accelerator, while a diffusion model fine-tuned through low-rank adaptation generates synthetic minority-class imagery. This synthetic output is automatically annotated, pseudo-labelled, by an intermediate detector and merged with classically augmented samples. The results show that the balanced dataset increases global mean average precision from 77.9% to 82.2%, with the minority class rising from F1=0.683 to F1=0.811, and that the quantised detector fits the on-chip memory and projects 25-30 frames per second on orbit. This approach contrasts with the conventional bent-pipe architecture, in which the satellite acts as a passive data collector. Therefore, the computational tests support the proposed workflow as a decision-support tool for real-time, autonomous airborne surveillance from nanosatellites.

</details>


### [127] [A Fuzzy Rule-based Neuro-Symbolic Approach for Pipe Severity Prediction in Sewer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28481)
*Ngoc Thai Le,Thanh Ma,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个模块化的模糊规则神经符号框架，将Swin Transformer神经感知与决策树转化的IF-THEN模糊规则推理解耦，用于自动化污水管道严重度评估，在五个高度不均衡的严重度类别上相比纯图像分类在准确率、平衡准确率、Macro F1和MCC上分别提升17.9%、12.2%、23.0%和17.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自动化污水管道严重度评估依赖直接图像分类，形成"黑箱"——视觉缺陷与最终严重度评分之间的关联是隐含的，缺乏可解释的推理路径。本研究旨在通过解耦神经感知与符号推理来弥补这一可追溯性缺口。

Method: 该框架包含两大模块：(1)感知模块使用Swin Transformer直接从图像预测14个多标签检查CODE等级；(2)推理模块使用决策树（Weka J48）在地面真值CODE与严重度标签上训练，其路径转换为19条固定IF-THEN规则。推理通过模糊逻辑进行：CODE条件的t-norm激活按规则置信度加权，并配合对应的s-norm组合产生可解释的类别证据。评估了Product、Łukasiewicz和Hamacher三种算子对。地面真值标签由五个独立大语言模型分析原始检查员笔记后通过共识生成，数据集包含3,244张图像，涵盖五个高度不均衡的严重度类别。

Result: 相比纯图像分类，框架在准确率、平衡准确率、Macro F1和MCC上分别提升17.9%、12.2%、23.0%和17.3%。框架在保持竞争力的类别平衡性能的同时，提供了从预测CODE等级到规则支持和严重度证据的可追溯推理。

Conclusion: 该神经符号框架在取得competitive整体性能的同时实现了推理过程的可解释性和可追溯性，有效弥合了视觉缺陷与严重度评分之间的语义鸿沟，为污水管道评估提供了兼顾准确性与透明性的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard automated sewer pipe severity assessment relies on direct image classification, creating a "black box" where the link between visual defects and final severity scores remains implicit. This study introduces a modular, fuzzy rule-based neuro-symbolic framework that bridges this gap by decoupling neural perception from symbolic reasoning. The perception module utilizes a Swin Transformer to predict 14 multilabel inspection CODE degrees directly from images. For reasoning, a DT, specifically Weka's J48, algorithm is trained on ground-truth CODEs and severity labels, and its paths are converted into 19 fixed IF--THEN rules. Inference operates via fuzzy logic: t-norm activations from CODE conditions are weighted by rule confidence and combined with corresponding s-norms to produce interpretable class evidence. We assessed Product, Łukasiewicz, and Hamacher operator pairs using a dataset of 3,244 images spanning five highly imbalanced severity classes. Ground-truth labels were robustly generated via consensus from five independent large language models analyzing original inspector notes. Our results show an improvement of accuracy, balanced accuracy, Macro F1 and MCC by 17.9%, 12.2%, 23.0%, and 17.3%, respectively, over image-only based classification.
  Overall, the framework combines competitive class-balanced performance with traceable reasoning from predicted CODE degrees to rule supports and severity evidence.

</details>


### [128] [SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28488)
*Yunhao Liang,Xianqi Cao,Pujun Zhang,Yuan Qu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE框架，将供应链规整计划（品类、货源分配、补货频率、配送路线）建模为复合策略模型，通过共享操作表示和端到端效用评估实现跨部门、跨决策环节的统一协调优化，并在叮咚买菜和京东的真实数据上验证其优于分阶段优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链中，完整的补货计划涉及品类选择、货源分配、补货频率和配送路线等多类决策，这些决策在运营上高度耦合（品类影响需求与负载，货源与频率重塑配送请求，路线成本决定前期决策价值）。然而这些决策往往由不同部门、不同系统分别优化，导致缺货、库存风险和运输浪费。因此需要一种能统一协调这些耦合决策的端到端方法。

Method: SCOPE框架将供应链实体表示为token，通过共享的操作表示进行上下文化，并将每种token类型映射到对应的决策接口。每个决策基于先前决策形成的部分计划逐步构建，最终完整计划通过共享的系统级效用函数来评估。在都市生鲜零售场景中实例化，覆盖服务频率、品类、容量压力和路网路由之间的强交互。

Result: 在叮咚买菜和京东两个大规模供应链的真实运营数据上，SCOPE在两种补货梯队场景下均持续优于独立分阶段优化方法及实务导向的基线条线，证明了跨部门协调学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 学习和协调跨部门的运营耦合能够带来更有效的端到端供应链决策，验证了从孤立的决策模块向统一操作规划迈进的可行性。

Abstract: Can supply-chain AI move beyond isolated decision modules toward unified operational planning? A complete replenishment plan specifies which products each location carries, which upstream facility supplies it, how often it is replenished, and how deliveries are routed. These decisions are operationally coupled: the selected assortment changes the demand and load passed to later stages; source assignment and replenishment frequency reshape the delivery requests; and route feasibility and cost, in turn, determine the system value of the earlier choices. Yet in modern supply chains, these decisions are often handled by separate departments and optimized through separate systems, which can lead to stockouts, inventory exposure, and avoidable transportation. We propose SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination, a composite policy model that represents supply-chain entities as tokens, contextualizes them through a shared operational representation, and maps each token type to the corresponding decision interface. Each decision builds on the partial plan formed by earlier decisions while the completed plan is evaluated using a shared system-level utility. We instantiate this framework in urban fresh-retail replenishment, where service frequency, assortment, capacity pressure, and road-network routing interact strongly, and evaluate it on real operational data from Dingdong and JD.com, two large-scale supply chains operating at different replenishment echelons. Across both settings, SCOPE consistently outperforms methods that optimize each decision stage separately, as well as practice-oriented baselines commonly used in supply-chain operations. These results show that learning and coordinating cross-department operational couplings lead to more effective end-to-end supply-chain decisions.

</details>


### [129] [InfoOps Bench: A live information operations safety benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28503)
*Dorian Quelle,Lisa-Maria Neudert,Jonathan Bright,John Gallacher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个动态更新的AI基准测试（InfoOpsBench），用于评估前沿大语言模型被国家支持的信息操作（如俄罗斯、中国、伊朗）所利用的风险。测试了8家供应商的17个模型，发现大多数模型可被诱导用于信息操作，诚信分数（拒绝请求的百分比）从8.8%到94.5%不等，且与模型大小无关。此外，中国开发的模型在面对与中国相关的批评性主张时拒绝率显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被广泛部署，它们可能被国家支持的信息操作所利用，成为传播虚假或偏动性内容的工具。现有基准测试多为静态的，容易被模型饱和或过拟合，缺乏对动态、实时演化的信息操作威胁的持续性评估能力。作者希望通过一个活跃更新的基准来持续衡量和监控前沿模型在这方面的脆弱性。

Method: 研究构建了一个动态AI基准，从实时监控管道中采集超过2,100个来自俄罗斯、中国和伊朗的国家支持信息操作样本。基准测试覆盖8家供应商的17个模型，采用四种提示框架进行测试，并配套每周更新的网站追踪最突出的信息主张。诚信分数定义为模型拒绝请求的百分比，用以衡量模型抵抗信息操作的能力。

Result: 研究发现：大多数模型可以被诱导参与信息操作；诚信分数范围从8.8%到94.5%，差距达85.7个百分点且与模型规模无关；模型选择会影响信息操作的性质——有些模型会捏造细节使输出比原始材料更有害，有些则在合规的同时化解主张；事实核查率从2.9%到72.9%不等；中国开发的模型（除GLM 5.2外）在面对基于事实的中国批评性主张时合规度急剧下降，相对匹配的良性主张下降了48-70个百分点。

Conclusion: 模型对信息操作的抵御能力至少部分与对良性主张的拒绝倾向相关，这揭示了模型可用性与安全性之间的平衡挑战。模型大小并非决定信息操作抵御能力的关键因素，且不同模型在信息操作中的行为特征差异显著。该动态基准的设计使其能够抵抗测试饱和问题，为持续评估模型完整性提供了可行框架。

Abstract: In this paper we present an active, constantly updated AI benchmark which measures the integrity of frontier language models against being co-opted for state-backed information operations. We draw on over 2,100 information operations from a live monitoring pipeline which tracks Russian, Chinese and Iranian state-backed information assets. Alongside this paper, we release a companion website that tracks the most prominent claims spread by state-backed media outlets, updated weekly, available from: pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench. The dynamic nature of the benchmark makes it resistant to saturation.
  In the benchmark, we test 17 models from 8 providers across four prompt framings. We find that most models can be co-opted for information operations. Integrity scores, defined as the percentage of refused requests, range from 8.8% to 94.5%, an 85.7-percentage-point spread not explained by model size. Model choice also changes the character of the resulting operation. Some models fabricate details and produce output more harmful than the source material, others defuse claims even while complying, and fact-checking rates vary from 2.9% to 72.9%.
  Integrity against information operations is at least partly related to refusal to produce content even for benign claims, illustrating the challenge of balancing model usability with safety. With one exception (Z.ai's GLM 5.2), the Chinese-developed models sharply cut compliance on factually grounded but China-critical claims, dropping 48-70 percentage points relative to matched benign claims.

</details>


### [130] [Selective Credibility-Limited Belief Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28523)
*Theofanis Aravanis,Costas D. Koutras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性可信度受限信念更新框架，在Katsuno-Mendelzon更新与可信度受限更新基础上，允许将复合认知输入按源世界转化为更弱的代理后再执行更新，从而支持部分实现复合输入的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信度受限信念更新方法将认知输入视为不可分割的整体，无法表示仅部分复合认知输入可实现的情形。Katsuno-Mendelzon更新假设认知输入可以从每个初始可能世界完全并入，缺乏对可信域的限制。

Method: 提出选择性可信度受限信念更新（selective credibility-limited belief update），核心思想是在可信度受限转换之前，将认知输入相对于每个源世界转化为一个更弱的代理（proxy）。提供了该更新算子类的语义与公理化刻画，并识别两个良行为的子类：一致性保持更新算子（要求一致的原认知输入转换后的代理必须可信）和极大一致性保持更新算子（要求所选代理在原认知输入的可信后果中信息量最大）。

Result: 证明了可信度受限信念更新是该框架的特例，而Katsuno-Mendelzon信念更新在移除可信度限制且变换函数为恒等时也可被恢复为特例。表明该框架统一且严格更具表达力，支持依赖源世界的选择性接受。

Conclusion: 提出的框架统一涵盖了既有信念更新方法，并提供了更强的表达能力，能够支持仅部分实现复合认知输入及源相关的选择性接受等更复杂场景。

Abstract: Belief update concerns changes in an agent's beliefs induced by changes in the underlying world. Standard Katsuno-Mendelzon update assumes that an epistemic input can be incorporated from every initially possible world, whereas credibility-limited belief update restricts, for each source world, the successor worlds regarded as credible or reachable. Nevertheless, existing credibility-limited approaches treat the epistemic input as an indivisible whole, and therefore cannot represent cases in which only part of a compound epistemic input can be realized. We introduce selective credibility-limited belief update, in which the epistemic input is transformed, relative to each source world, into a weaker proxy before the credibility-limited transition is performed. We provide semantic and axiomatic characterizations of the resulting class of update operators. We then identify two well-behaved sub-classes; namely, consistency-preserving update operators, which require every transformed epistemic input to be credible from its source world whenever the original epistemic input is consistent, and maximal consistency-preserving update operators, which additionally require the selected proxy to be maximally informative among the credible consequences of the original epistemic input. Finally, we establish the generality of the proposed framework by showing that credibility-limited belief update is recovered as a special case, while Katsuno--Mendelzon belief update emerges when credibility restrictions are removed and the transformation functions are taken to be identities. These results demonstrate that the framework provides a unified and strictly more expressive account of belief update, encompassing established approaches while supporting source-dependent selective acceptance.

</details>


### [131] [MANTA: Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation for Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28527)
*Mao-xun Huang,Jerry Wang,Yi-Cheng Lai,Zhengxin Zhang,Claire Cardie,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MANTA是一个多智能体网络拓扑自适应框架，允许通信结构在推理时自我进化。它在执行前初始化任务条件拓扑，并在部署期间监控协作痕迹，当当前组织结构不足时应用有界结构更新（可修改角色、通信链路、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径），同时保留任务接口和智能体预算。在五个基准上平均得分74.0，比最强基线高出5.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统通常将通信拓扑视为固定设计选择或离线优化目标，缺乏在推理时自适应调整通信结构的能力，限制了系统应对复杂动态任务的灵活性和效率。

Method: MANTA框架包含两个阶段：执行前，根据先前的结构经验初始化任务条件拓扑；部署期间，持续监控协作痕迹，并在当前组织不再充分时应用有界的结构更新。这些更新涵盖智能体角色、通信链接、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径的修改，但保持任务接口和智能体预算不变。

Result: 在信息寻求、工具使用、规划、工作流执行和数学推理五个基准上，MANTA达到74.0的最高平均得分，超越最强基线5.8个百分点，并在PlanCraft上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 推理时的自我改进可以扩展到协作架构本身，即多智能体系统的通信拓扑结构可以支持动态自适应演化，这为提升复杂问题求解能力提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through task decomposition, agent specialization, information exchange, and intermediate validation. However, existing systems typically treat communication topology as a fixed design choice or an offline optimization target. We introduce MANTA, a framework for Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation that enables communication structures to self-evolve at inference time. Before execution, MANTA initializes a task-conditioned topology from prior structural experience. During deployment, it monitors collaboration traces and applies bounded structural updates when the current organization becomes insufficient. These updates can modify agent roles, communication links, execution order, information visibility, and validation pathways while preserving the task interface and agent budget. We evaluate MANTA against representative single-agent and multi-agent baselines on five benchmarks spanning information seeking, tool use, planning, workflow execution, and mathematical reasoning. MANTA achieves the highest average score of 74.0, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.8 percentage points and obtaining the best result on PlanCraft. These results show that inference-time self-improvement can extend to the architecture of collaboration itself.

</details>


### [132] [Rethinking Inference-Time Scaling in Local Computer-Use Agents: Failure Modes and Compute Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28573)
*Woongkyu Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.AI
